Testimony","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_800ac8d5c4e798e54804","text":"Trust grants standing to a witness or arbiter, delegated judgment gives that standing practical force, and attribution of truth or falsehood determines whether the entrusted word remains authoritative.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Combat and the Truth of a Charge","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_801cbdbaba31bc81a40c","text":"Collective life regulates opposition through confrontation, negotiated settlement, adjudication, office, custom, and governance.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Rank, Aptitude, and Counsel","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_85c239109eeada523fa6","text":"Social position is evaluated through low rank, practical aptitude, penetrating counsel, and failure of judgment.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Pupil Image and Sightless Intact Eye","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_85f05894fd52e8ecd0b7","text":"Perception establishes the active sensory function and the pupil gives vision a visible miniature human image. The standing eye reverses that relation by preserving outward form after sight disappears, while the surface mark reinforces the distinction between what a body displays and what it can do.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Trained Work Animal and Lagging Failure","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_877c55597da6b0bcf883","text":"An animal bred or habituated for work continues under load, while dullness, weakness, or exhaustion makes it lag and halt.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Residence, City, and Civic Obedience","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_8877943e3e8f4db2809a","text":"95:3 `البلد`; 95:4 `تقويم`; 95:7 `الدين`; 95:8 `أليس`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Faith, Good Work, and Beneficence","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_89228032c3865f03f4e3","text":"Faith supplies settled assent, work converts assent into action, and rectitude defines the action as sound. Beneficence carries the process beyond avoidance of فساد into active good done well.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Edge, Enclosure, and Near-Far Relation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_8b457e585aa87802d139","text":"The aligned edge gives a place shape, the enclosure creates an inside and a vicinity, and approach measures movement toward that boundary. Distance names the corresponding movement away from it.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Inquiry, Urging, and Handover","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_92cf6d53c4d1b918a584","text":"Small utterance forms organize discourse by pointing, questioning, urging, excluding, negating, calling, and answering.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Pointing, Reference, and Naming","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_94097457f044a5ec74ca","text":"The attention particle opens a shared field of notice, the demonstrative selects a present item, and relative or proper naming keeps that item identifiable in discourse. `هذا` realizes the compact pointing form on the surface.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Pointing, Reference, and Naming","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_9ca079d34f489d7dbf6c","text":"Small utterance forms organize discourse by pointing, questioning, urging, excluding, negating, calling, and answering.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Pupil Image and Sightless Intact Eye","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_9d700cf9dfe6b2265043","text":"sensory perception by sight, hearing, or feeling `ء ن س:B002/m01`, reflected human in the pupil `ء ن س:B005/m01`, intact but sightless eye `ق و م:B021/m01`, bodily surface mark `ب ل د:B006/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Divine Address, Amen, and Vocal Response","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_a16c0f8daf1d1e94c7da","text":"Divine address establishes the addressee, the answering voice realizes response, and `amen` turns response into a request for fulfillment. Confusion is the participant's corresponding state when return no longer advances the process.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Office, Custom, and Civic Stewardship","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_a6747b39f5c17dfcc808","text":"Settlement supplies the governed setting, office assigns responsibility, custom stabilizes repeated conduct, and stewardship keeps the arrangement functioning over time.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Residence, City, and Civic Obedience","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_a93732e0735a0d15297d","text":"Residence converts land into an inhabited station, prolonged remaining stabilizes it, and civic obedience gives the settlement an ordered political form. City is therefore both a place occupied and a relation maintained.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Edge, Enclosure, and Near-Far Relation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_adb4d79004e45cda235a","text":"95:2 `طور`; 95:3 `البلد`; 95:7 `بعد`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Nest, Egg, and Young Animals","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_aec844d278310d94785e","text":"Animal life is organized through trained labor, bodily continuity, milk production, movement, and the protection of young.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000715/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Rank, Aptitude, and Counsel","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_b1991e5a5efe91117f1c","text":"low social rank `س ف ل:B002/m01`, dullness and lagging capacity `ب ل د:B007/m01`, penetrating counsel `ب ع د:B008/m01`, weak opinion `ل ي س:B007/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Rank, Aptitude, and Counsel","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_b22bc48baf35b7f71b14","text":"Low rank and dull performance mark diminished standing, while penetrating counsel marks valued social capacity.
